  Q1 — Valeo (row 187): you selected Not specified. Rationale: CLAUDE.md §15

  is what earns it Not applicable.

  that row; filling it would be imputation of the same class the protocol
  forbids with fillna(0). The pattern gets recorded in
  NORMALIZATION_REPORT_v3.md rather than acted on.

❯ I am approving one explicit protocol amendment before Phase 2 is frozen.      
                                                                                
  Update the protocol so that real non-Georgia Location values are valid        
  geographic data and may later produce derived city/county values.             
                                                                                
  The new rule is:                                                              
                                                                                
  Real Location, Georgia or non-Georgia                                         
  → preserve the Location                                                       
  → derive city/county later when supported by that Location value              
                                                                                
  Missing Location                                                              
  → Not specified                                                               
  → city/county = NULL                                                          
                                                                                
  Structurally inapplicable Location                                            
  → Not applicable                                                              
  → city/county = NULL                                                          
                                                                                
  Important: do not substitute Address for Location when deriving facility      
  geography. A real corporate/out-of-state address may be preserved as the      
  factual Address, but if Location is Not applicable or Not specified, derived  
  facility city/county remain NULL.                                             
                                                                                
  Therefore:                                                                    
                                                                                
  Valeo:                                                                        
  Location = Not specified                                                      
  Address = Not specified                                                       
                                                                                
  Volvo exception:                                                              
  preserve the real NJ/NC Address                                               
  Location remains Not applicable                                               
  derived facility city/county remain NULL                                      
                                                                                
  Make this as a formal, versioned protocol amendment in README.md and record   
  the amendment and rationale in PROVENANCE_v3.md.                              
                                                                                
  State explicitly that this amendment was made before Phase 2 was frozen and   
  before any model results, test/Q42 inference, holdout selection, or           
  downstream evaluation was observed.                                           
                                                                                
  Update only the affected geographic semantics; do not redesign any other part 
  of the protocol.                                                              
                                                                                
  After making the amendment, show me the exact protocol diff and stop for      
  approval before continuing Phase 2.

     │ ## A-001 — Geographic semantics: real non-Georgia locations are valid   │
     │ geographic data                                                         │
     │                                                                         │
     │ **Approved:** 2026-08-23 · **Status:** ACTIVE · **Supersedes:** the     │
     │ geographic clause of Phase 2 and                                        │
     │ the `city`/`county` parsing clause of Phase 4.                          │
     │                                                                         │
     │ **Timing.** Made **before Phase 2 was frozen** and before any canonical │
     │ records existed — and before                                            │
     │ any model result, test or Q42 inference, holdout selection, or          │
     │ downstream evaluation had been                                          │
     │ observed. Test and Q42 remain sealed (`LOCKED_UNTIL_PHASE_40`). No      │
     │ measured outcome informed it.                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ **Problem.** The original wording made derived `city`/`county`          │
     │ conditional on a location being *a                                      │
     │ real Georgia location*, conflating **reality** with **Georgia-ness**.   │
     │ It also left a genuinely                                                │
     │ unknown `Location` (blank `Location` *and* blank `Address`) with no     │
     │ defined sentinel, since the only                                        │
     │ named exception was Volvo.                                              │
     │                                                                         │
     │ **The amended rule.**                                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ | `Location` | `Location` value | derived `city`/`county` |             │
     │ |---|---|---|                                                           │
     │ | real (Georgia **or** non-Georgia) | preserved as-is | derived later   │
     │ where that `Location` supports it |                                     │
     │ | missing / unknown | `Not specified` | SQL `NULL` |                    │
     │ | structurally inapplicable | `Not applicable` | SQL `NULL` |           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ **`Address` is never substituted for `Location`** when deriving         │
     │ facility geography. A real corporate                                    │
     │ or out-of-state `Address` is preserved as the factual `Address`, but    │
     │ when `Location` is                                                      │
     │ `Not applicable` or `Not specified`, derived facility `city`/`county`   │
     │ remain `NULL`. The sentinel                                             │
     │ never enters the `city`/`county` field.                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ **Consequences for the two named cases.**                               │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - **Valeo** (`row_id` 187) — `Location` blank and `Address` blank:      │
     │ `Location` = `Not specified`,                                           │
     │   `Address` = `Not specified`, derived `city`/`county` = `NULL`.        │
     │ - **Volvo** (`row_id` 192, 193) — the frozen exception is retained on   │
     │ the amended basis: the real                                             │
     │   NJ/NC `Address` values are preserved, `Location` remains `Not         │
     │ applicable`, and derived facility                                       │
     │   `city`/`county` remain `NULL`.                                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ **Scope limit.** A-001 changes geographic semantics only. It does       │
     │ **not** reinstate any retired v3                                        │
     │ component — no latitude/longitude, no geo training or evaluation, no    │
     │ distance/radius/nearest, no                                             │
     │ `probe_geo`, no geo SQL prompts. Those remain retired per `CLAUDE.md`   │
     │ §32.                                                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 3. README.md:113-114 — replace the Phase 2 geographic clause            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Replace exactly those two lines with:                                   │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Derived `city`/`county` follow **Amendment A-001**: a **real**          │
     │ `Location` — Georgia or non-Georgia —                                   │
     │ is preserved and may later yield derived `city`/`county` where that     │
     │ `Location` supports it; a                                               │
     │ **missing** `Location` becomes `Not specified` and a **structurally     │
     │ inapplicable** `Location` becomes                                       │
     │ `Not applicable`, both yielding `city`/`county` = SQL `NULL`. The       │
     │ sentinel never enters the                                               │

     │ `Location` when deriving facility                                       │

     OEM-reference guard — derived, no ambiguity

     Excluding the non-entity placeholders (Multiple OEMs 154, Not specified 17,
     Not applicable 9),
     25 rows carry entity-bearing primary_oems. Matching against the 193 exact
     company names yields
     11 exactly-matched companies:

     Archer Aviation Inc.      Blue Bird Corp.          Club Car LLC
     Hyundai Motor Group       Kia Georgia Inc.         Mercedes-Benz USA LLC
     Porsche Cars North America Inc.                    Rivian Automotive
     SK Battery America        Textron Specialized Vehicles
     Yamaha Motor Manufacturing Corp.

     Three values are composite/partial and match no company exactly: Blue Bird
     (3 rows),
     Hyundai Kia (6), Hyundai Kia Rivian (5). The derived set is invariant to 
     how they are parsed,
     because every constituent — Blue Bird Corp., Hyundai Motor Group, Kia 
     Georgia Inc.,
     Rivian Automotive — is independently referenced by an exact-match row
     elsewhere. So exact-only
     matching and full decomposition both yield the same 11. No guess is
     required and none is made; the
     implementation uses exact matching and asserts the invariance.

     11 matches the protocol's stated current count, but the gate is the
     placement rule, never
     len(...) == 11. The count is derived and reported, never asserted.

  4. Scope semantics must prevent future leakage                                
                                                                                
  The scientific contract is:                                                   
                                                                                
  training-data eligibility                                                     
  → determined using train_kb only                                              
                                                                                
  development evaluation                                                        
  → may use train_dev_kb as allowed by the protocol                             
                                                                                
  final test / deployed structured KB                                           
  → full_kb only when the protocol explicitly permits it                        
                                                                                
  Phase 4 itself should not generate training data or run evaluation, but the   
  loader must make later accidental leakage difficult.                          
                                                                                
  In particular:                                                                
                                                                                
  no training helper may silently access dev/test through full_kb;              
  no broad/global singleton containing all 205 records should be treated as the 
  default training KB;                                                          
  scope must be explicit at the API boundary;                                   
  later SQL prediction and gold execution must be able to use the same          
  requested scope;                                                              
  do not bake split membership into model-facing text.                          
                                                                                
  split_group is leakage-control metadata only and must never become answer     
  identity or normal model-visible content.                                     
                                                                                
  Exact company remains the answer/database identity.                           
                                                                                
  5. Geographic derivation under Amendment A-001                                
                                                                                
  Phase 4 may derive city and county only from geographic information           
  explicitly present in the canonical Location field.

  Address                                                                       
  external geocoding                                                            
  internet search                                                               
  company knowledge                                                             
  ZIP lookup                                                                    
  latitude/longitude                                                            
  nearest-city logic

  real Location, Georgia or non-Georgia                                         
  → preserve it                                                                 
  → derive only components explicitly represented there                         
                                                                                
  Location explicitly provides city but not county                              
  → city may be populated                                                       
  → county = NULL                                                               
                                                                                
  Location explicitly provides county but not city                              
  → county may be populated                                                     
  → city = NULL                                                                 
                                                                                
  Location = Not specified                                                      
  → city = NULL                                                                 
  → county = NULL                                                               
                                                                                
  Location = Not applicable                                                     
  → city = NULL                                                                 
  → county = NULL                                                               
                                                                                
  Do not infer missing components.                                              
                                                                                
  Address remains independent from Location.                                    
                                                                                
  Specifically preserve the frozen exceptions:

  Location = Not specified

  → city/county NULL

     Phase 4 — Loader and data contract

     Context

     Phase 3 is approved and frozen at cd34520. Phase 4 builds the single module
     that defines what a row
     means to everything downstream, and — critically — establishes the
     three-scope anti-leakage
     contract that every later generator and grader must go through. README.md
     Phase 4 is explicit:

     ▎ Three named KB scopes — the core anti-leakage contract. Every generator 
     ▎ and grader declares
     ▎ which scope it reads. There is no unscoped accessor.

     This is the phase where accidental leakage is made structurally difficult
     rather than merely
     discouraged. It also performs the city/county derivation that Amendment
     A-001 deferred here.

     Pre-flight verified (all green, read-only):

     branch                       main
     HEAD                         cd34520d8b27c97140c7331d355008f13a60ea96
     (matches)
     working tree                 clean
     canonical_records_v3.jsonl
     42851c0a2e93209ac5d37e73ce07447009e038b9db625004212722d7738e6488  (matches)
     company_split_groups_v3.csv
     a59d673ac9acafd1f70f60cf560491c32d8d11bacdab3c9bc5d77016b536b701  (DERIVED,
     == committed blob)
     canonical rows 205 · split rows 205 · row_id sets identical

     Scope arithmetic derived from the frozen split (numbers used only as
     validation, never as the
     construction mechanism):

     ┌──────────────┬──────┬─────────────────┬──────────────┐
     │    scope     │ rows │ exact companies │ split groups │
     ├──────────────┼──────┼─────────────────┼──────────────┤
     │ train_kb     │ 148  │ 141             │ 134          │
     ├──────────────┼──────┼─────────────────┼──────────────┤
     │ train_dev_kb │ 165  │ 158             │ 151          │
     ├──────────────┼──────┼─────────────────┼──────────────┤
     │ full_kb      │ 205  │ 193             │ 186          │
     └──────────────┴──────┴─────────────────┴──────────────┘

     Location parses unambiguously. All 202 real Location values are exactly
     City, X County (one
     comma each); zero unparseable; 61 distinct counties; no odd city tokens.
     The three sentinel rows
     (Valeo 187 Not specified; Volvo 192/193 Not applicable) yield NULL/NULL. So
     the "prefer NULL when
     ambiguous" branch is implemented but expected to fire zero times — which is
     itself reported.

     Implementation

     1. CLAUDE.md live status block — lines 44-46 ONLY

     CURRENT_PHASE: 4, LAST_COMPLETED_PHASE: 3, NEXT_PHASE: Phase 5 — Build 
     gnem_v3.sqlite`` (the
     exact README Phase 5 title, README.md:261). Line-targeted; the §37 example
     block stays frozen.

     2. finetune/kb_v3.py — the loader and data contract (the durable module)

     The one module later phases import. Key design points:

     - load_kb(scope) — scope is a required positional parameter with NO 
       default. Calling
       load_kb() raises TypeError; load_kb(None) or an unknown string raises
       ScopeError. There is
       no unscoped accessor and no module-level singleton holding all 205 rows.
     - Asserts the Phase 2 canonical hash and the Phase 3 split hash on every
       load; refuses on drift.
     - Maps all 18 canonical columns; employment kept as recorded and asserted
       populated;
       certification_count loaded for validation only.
     - derive_city_county(location) — reads only the row's own canonical
       Location. Never
       Address, geocoding, ZIP, lat/long, company knowledge, or any other row.
       Rules exactly as frozen:
       real Location → derive only the components explicitly present;
       city-without-county → county NULL;
       county-without-city → city NULL; Not specified / Not applicable → both
       NULL. Anything not
       unambiguously parseable → NULL for the unresolved component, and the case
       is recorded.
     - MODEL_FACING_FIELDS — an explicit projection that excludes
       certification_count,
       split, split_group, and can never contain latitude/longitude or graph
       metadata. Split
       membership selects rows; it never enters a record handed to a model.
     - static_check_no_training_generator_reaches_full_kb(paths) — the static
       check README Phase 4
       requires. Implemented now; it currently has zero training generators to
       scan (they arrive at Phase
       10+), and the audit will say exactly that rather than implying it
       verified something it did not.

     County form — judgment call. county is stored verbatim as it appears in 
     Location
     ("Hall County", not "Hall"). Stripping the type-word is a normalization no
     rule authorizes, and
     the Phase 2 correction established that unauthorized normalization is a
     defect. Both forms are
     trivially derivable from the other, so this is recoverable if Phase 5
     prefers the bare name.

     3. finetune/phase4_validate_loader.py — the phase gate

     Runs every gate, then writes the audit. Fails loudly and writes nothing on
     any breach.

     4. Artifact

     README.md Phase 4 names no data artifact — the module is the deliverable.
     One audit file is
     created because deterministic validation must be recorded and the user
     requires those contents:

     validation_v3/LOADER_CONTRACT_v3.md

     It records the canonical SHA, split SHA, loader version + SHA, scope
     definitions, realized
     row/company/group counts, derived and NULL city/county counts, ambiguous
     cases (expected zero),
     sentinel behaviour, and all validation results.

     Verification

     Scope gates: train_kb ⊂ train_dev_kb ⊂ full_kb; train_kb row_ids == Phase 3
     train exactly;
     train_dev_kb == train ∪ dev exactly; full_kb == all 205; no dev or test row
     in train_kb; no
     test row in train_dev_kb; all 40 test rows reachable only via full_kb; no
     exact company or
     split_group spans scopes (inherited from Phase 3, re-asserted here).

     Geography gates: every derived city/county is a literal substring of that
     row's own Location;
     derivation is byte-identical when Address is blanked out (proving
     independence); sentinels → NULL;
     Valeo/Volvo match A-001; no latitude/longitude field is produced anywhere;
     deterministic across runs;
     no cross-row inference (each row derived in isolation).

     Preservation gates: row_id, company, employment, location, address,
     processes,
     services, certifications byte-equal to canonical — including the AVS row 16
     trailing space.

     Fault injection (must fail, write nothing): load_kb() with no scope;
     load_kb("full");
     load_kb(None); a scope built from the wrong splits; canonical or split SHA
     drift; a model-facing
     projection that leaks split_group or certification_count; a derivation
     attempt that reads
     Address.

     Determinism: two runs byte-identical. Tree clean; only Phase 4 files
     committed.

     Out of scope (Phase 5+)

     gnem_v3.sqlite, the companies table and the certification/process/service
     child tables (README
     assigns these to Phase 5), holdout registry, exposure ledger, value-holdout
     cost, training
     examples, CPT/factual/SQL data, dev sets, probes, Q42, inference, adapters,
     training, routing, graph
     structures. Test/Q42 remain LOCKED_UNTIL_PHASE_40. The frozen Phase 3 split
     is not modified.

  training generators silently see all 205 rows.                                
                                                                                
  If Phase 5 introduces SQL access/view logic, it must follow the exact scope   
  mechanism specified by README.                                                
                                                                                
  Later SQL gold and SQL prediction must ultimately be executable against the   
  same requested knowledge scope.                                               
                                                                                
  Do not make full_kb an implicit training default.                             
                                                                                
  split_group remains leakage-control metadata only and must not become         
  ordinary model-facing database knowledge.                                     
                                                                                
  12. Database validation                                                       
                                                                                
  Add/reuse gates proving at minimum:                                           
                                                                                
  database created from frozen Phase 4 contract                                 
  companies rows preserve all 205 canonical records                             
  row_id unique in companies                                                    
  exact company strings preserved                                               
                                                                                
  child-table foreign keys valid                                                
  no orphan certifications                                                      
  no orphan processes                                                           
  no orphan services                                                            
                                                                                
  child rows trace back to canonical row_id                                     
  certification sentinel creates zero child rows                                
  canonical process/service/certification terms preserved                       
                                                                                
  Employment preserved                                                          
  city/county match Phase 4 derivation exactly                                  
                                                                                
  no latitude/longitude                                                         
  no graph tables                                                               
  no unauthorized normalization                                                 
                                                                                
  Report actual:                                                                
                                                                                
  company-table rows                                                            
  certification child rows                                                      
  certification-covered rows/companies as appropriate                           
  process child rows                                                            
  process-covered rows/companies                                                
  service child rows                                                            
  service-covered rows/companies                                                
  table names                                                                   
  column schemas                                                                
  foreign-key relationships                                                     
  indexes, if Phase 5 authorizes them                                           
                                                                                
  Derive these counts from the database and canonical source. Do not hard-code  
  them as construction logic.                                                   
                                                                                
  13. Round-trip/source equivalence                                             
                                                                                
  Where practical, validate a deterministic relational round trip:              
                                                                                
  companies + child tables                                                      
        ↓                                                                       
  reconstruct canonical multivalue facts                                        
        ↓

  The reconstruction should reproduce the canonical

  Sentinel certification rows should reconstruct as the sentinel/zero-evidence  
  state according to the contract, not as a literal credential.                 
                                                                                
  14. SQLite integrity                                                          
                                                                                
  Run SQLite integrity checks such as those required by README, including       
  foreign-key enforcement where appropriate.

     /plan to edit
     Phase 5 — Build gnem_v3.sqlite

     Context

     Phase 4 is approved and frozen at 67127ef. Phase 5 turns the canonical
     records into the relational
     knowledge base the SQL arms will query. README.md Phase 5 gives the reason
     precisely: multi-valued
     fields cannot be queried with WHERE col = 'value' against a ;-joined cell,
     so child tables make
     membership a clean equality join and keep the JOIN-learning test alive.

     The critical requirement is that the database is scoped, not singular —
     README is blunt that "SQL
     must honour them or the scoping is fiction," because every gold in the task
     pool is produced by
     executing a query, so a single all-rows database would compute
     train_kb_gold over test data.

     Pre-flight verified (read-only, all green):

     branch main · HEAD 67127ef401eb5774f96f3a0a11aad02439c2a069 · tree clean
     canonical_records_v3.jsonl   42851c0a…6488   (matches)
     company_split_groups_v3.csv  a59d673a…b701   (matches)
     finetune/kb_v3.py            67207659…14ba   (matches)
     Phase 4 gate re-run: all gates pass · sqlite3 3.45.1

     Derived child-row counts match README's expectations exactly (derived from
     canonical, not
     hard-coded — README says "≈"):

     ┌────────────────┬──────┬────────────┬────────────────────┐
     │  child table   │ rows │ vocabulary │ README expectation │
     ├────────────────┼──────┼────────────┼────────────────────┤
     │ processes      │ 555  │ 49         │ ≈555 / ≈49         │
     ├────────────────┼──────┼────────────┼────────────────────┤
     │ services       │ 290  │ 22         │ ≈290 / ≈22         │
     ├────────────────┼──────┼────────────┼────────────────────┤
     │ certifications │ 662  │ 59         │ ≈662 / ≈59         │
     └────────────────┴──────┴────────────┴────────────────────┘

     34 rows carry the None identified after search sentinel and all have
     certification_count == 0,
     so they must produce zero certification child rows.

     Implementation

     1. CLAUDE.md live status block — lines 44-46 ONLY

     CURRENT_PHASE: 5, LAST_COMPLETED_PHASE: 4, NEXT_PHASE: Phase 6 — Context 
     renderer and token budget (exact README title, README.md:305).
     Line-targeted; §37 example block untouched.

     2. finetune/phase5_build_sqlite.py

     Builds through the approved Phase 4 contract — kb_v3.load_kb("full_kb") —
     so there is no second
     normalization pipeline and no re-parse of geography. Refuses to run on any
     frozen-hash drift before
     writing a single byte.

     Four tables, exactly as README specifies:

     companies(row_id PK, company, category, industry_group, location, address,
               primary_facility_type, ev_supply_chain_role, primary_oems,
               supplier_or_affiliation_type, employment, product_or_service,
               ev_battery_relevant, classification_method, city, county)      --
     scalars only
     certifications(row_id -> companies.row_id, company, standard_family)
     processes(row_id -> companies.row_id, company, process)
     services(row_id -> companies.row_id, company, service)

     companies columns are derived as kb_v3.MODEL_FACING_FIELDS minus the three
     multi-valued fields —
     i.e. straight from the Phase 4 contract, not a fresh field list.
     certification_count is not a
     column anywhere: README says a model that can COUNT the child table must
     not be handed the
     answer. split / split_group are likewise absent — leakage metadata never
     becomes a queryable
     fact.

     Row identity preserved. row_id is the primary key and every child row
     carries its parent
     row_id, so the 205 records never collapse into 193 companies. The 9
     repeated-company rows stay
     distinct records, and child facts attach to the row that stated them.

     Terms are taken verbatim from the already-canonical Phase 2 values — split
     on "; " only. No
     re-sorting, renaming, stemming, synonym-mapping, merging, or further
     spelling normalization.

     3. Scoped views — the anti-leakage mechanism

     README says "three read-only scoped views over the same four tables".
     Judgment call to flag: I
     read that as three scoped view-sets, and will create 12 views
     ({scope}_companies,
     {scope}_certifications, {scope}_processes, {scope}_services for each of the
     three scopes).
     Scoping only companies would leave SELECT COUNT(*) FROM processes returning
     all 205 rows' terms —
     exactly the fiction README warns about. Every table a scope exposes must be
     scoped.

     Scope membership is embedded as a row_id IN (…) filter derived from the
     frozen Phase 3 split. No
     split or split_group column is created, so split membership is never
     queryable as data. Views are
     inherently read-only (no INSTEAD OF triggers).

     Not in this phase: run_sql, scope-neutral name resolution, and the
     refusal-without-scope
     executor belong to Phase 7 (README.md:290). Phase 5 creates the scoped
     views the Phase 7
     executor will select between; it exposes no query API at all, so it cannot
     become an unscoped
     convenience path.

     4. Artifacts

     datasets_v3/gnem_v3.sqlite            four tables + 12 scoped views
     validation_v3/DB_VALIDATION_v3.md     schema printout, hashes, counts, all
     gate results

     Verification

     Structure: 205 companies rows; row_id unique; exact company strings
     byte-equal to canonical
     (all 193 distinct names, 205 rows, no collapse); employment preserved;
     city/county byte-equal to the
     Phase 4 derivation (compared against kb_v3 output, not re-derived).

     Child tables: every child row's row_id resolves to a companies row — zero
     orphans, checked
     both by PRAGMA foreign_key_check and by an explicit anti-join; the 34
     sentinel rows produce zero
     certification rows; term text byte-equal to the canonical split.

     Round trip: reconstruct each row's processes / services / certifications
     string from the
     child tables and compare against the frozen canonical records — sentinel
     rows must reconstruct to
     None identified after search, not to a literal credential.

     Scope gates: each scoped view returns the expected row count (148 / 165 /
     205 companies);
     train_kb provably excludes every dev and test split group; child-table
     views are scoped in step
     with their parent; no view exposes split, split_group, certification_count,
     latitude or
     longitude.

     Prohibitions: exactly four base tables — no graph_edges, no coordinates, no
     OEM relationship
     table, no spatial index; grep proves no gnem.sqlite fallback anywhere in
     the repo.

     Integrity: PRAGMA integrity_check and PRAGMA foreign_key_check clean.

     Determinism: rebuild twice into temp paths and compare. Report
     byte-identity only if actually
     observed; otherwise report logical/table-level equivalence and say so
     plainly.

     Fault injection (must fail, write nothing): frozen-hash drift; an injected
     orphan child row; a
     sentinel row emitting a certification child row; a scoped view returning
     the wrong row count; a
     certification_count or split_group column reaching the schema.

     Out of scope (Phase 6+)

     Context renderer and token budget (Phase 6), run_sql/grading (Phase 7),
     evaluation stack (Phase 8),
     holdout registry, exposure ledger, value-holdout cost, training datasets,
     CPT/factual/SQL data,
     probes, Q42, predictions, inference, adapters, training, routers, graph
     structures. Test/Q42 remain
     LOCKED_UNTIL_PHASE_40. The Phase 4 loader is not modified.

● User answered Claude's questions:
  ⎿  · BLOCKING: the real Qwen tokenizer files are cached locally, but 
     transformers/tokenizers aren't installed in this Python. The only env that 
     has transformers (5.14.1) is /home/sreeja/GNEM-Router-RAG-pipeline/.venv-ft

     dependency on the old repository path. How should I get the tokenizer? → 
     Repo-local .venv-v3 (Recommended)
     · Phase 6 must freeze the evaluator's max_seq_length for the budget 
     equation usable_context = min(model_configured_context, 
     evaluator_max_seq_length). What value? → 32768 — match model (Recommended)
     · Phase 6 must also freeze max_new_tokens, which is reserved from the 
     budget before any prompt is accepted. Note CLAUDE.md §25: generation ending
     at max_new_tokens becomes `truncated_output` — an under-provisioned value 
     turns legitimate long answers into failures. → 1024 (Recommended)

     │    with transformers is /home/sreeja/GNEM-Router-RAG-pipeline/.venv-ft, │

     │    repository (HEAD = b18313ae…), and CLAUDE.md §7 forbids a runtime    │
     │    dependency on that path.                                             │
     │ 2. Evaluator max_seq_length = 32768 — no artificial ceiling below the   │
     │    model's real context.                                                │
     │ 3. max_new_tokens = 1024 — ample for C_answers set results, so a        │
     │    genuine long answer is not                                           │
     │    mislabelled truncated_output (CLAUDE.md §25).                        │
     │                                                                         │
     │ tokenizer advertised    131072   NOT USED                               │
     │ model configured         32768   config.json max_position_embeddings    │
     │ evaluator max_seq_length 32768   frozen here                            │
     │ usable_context           32768   = min(model_configured,                │
     │ evaluator_max_seq_length)                                               │
     │ max_new_tokens            1024   reserved before any prompt is accepted │
     │ max input tokens         31744   = usable_context - max_new_tokens      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Worst-case rendered row is ~908 characters (~260 tokens), so the margin │
     │ is ~31k tokens. Real token                                              │
     │ counts will replace that estimate once the tokenizer is available.      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Implementation                                                          │
     │                                                                         │

     │                                                                         │
     │ 2. .gitignore — add .venv-v3/                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 3. finetune/context_renderer_v3.py                                      │
     │                                                                         │
     │ - render_oracle_context(scope, row_id) — scope is a required positional │
     │   parameter, no default.                                                │
     │   Unknown/None scope raises ScopeError; a row_id outside the requested  │
     │   scope raises                                                          │
     │   ScopeError, so scope="train_kb", row_id=<test row> cannot return the  │
     │   test row.                                                             │
     │ - Reads through the Phase 4/5 contract (kb_v3), reusing                 │
     │   MODEL_FACING_FIELDS. Forbidden fields                                 │
     │   (split, split_group, certification_count, latitude, longitude, graph  │
     │   metadata) are                                                         │
     │   structurally unreachable, not filtered out afterwards.                │
     │ - Frozen rendering spec (recorded in the audit): fixed field order,     │
     │   fixed labels, ": "                                                    │
     │   separator, "; " between child terms, child facts in the Phase 5       │
     │   canonical case-insensitive                                            │
     │   order — never SQLite's natural row order — sentinels rendered as      │
     │   their frozen canonical strings,                                       │
     │   and no trailing-whitespace normalization of canonical values.         │
     │ - A company with no certification evidence renders the frozen None      │
     │   identified after search                                               │
     │   representation; no certification child fact is manufactured.          │
     │ - build_ctx_oracle_prompt(scope, row_id, question) returns the complete │
     │   chat message list, so                                                 │
     │   measurement covers system + question + context + instructions +       │
     │   chat-template control tokens —                                        │
     │   not just the context block.                                           │
     │ - RENDERER_VERSION plus a self-hash recorded in the audit.              │
     │ - Physical scoped-view names (train_kb_companies, …) never appear in    │
     │   any prompt.                                                           │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 4. finetune/phase6_context_budget.py                                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Loads the real tokenizer from the local cache at the pinned revision    │
     │ (local_files_only), refusing                                            │
     │ to substitute anything if unavailable. Reads the model's context from   │
     │ config.json rather than                                                 │
     │ hard-coding it, and asserts it equals 32768. Applies the real chat      │
     │ template. Measures every one of                                         │
     │ the 205 rows as deterministic validation fixtures — this is rendering   │
     │ existing KB rows, not probe                                             │
     │ generation: no probe file, no gold answer, no generated QA is written.  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Records per prompt family: name, applicability, case count,             │
     │ min/median/p95/max input tokens,                                        │
     │ max_new_tokens, worst-case total, usable context, margin, PASS/FAIL.    │
     │ Only base_ctx_oracle is                                                 │
     │ implemented and measured; future families are listed as to be checked   │
     │ when introduced, never                                                  │
     │ fabricated.                                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 5. finetune/phase6_fault_tests.py                                       │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Omitted scope · invalid scope · row outside requested scope · prompt    │
     │ over budget · attempted silent                                          │
     │ truncation · forbidden metadata in context · gold answer in context ·   │
     │ gold SQL in context ·                                                   │
     │ generated-QA text in context · frozen DB/hash drift.                    │
     │                                                                         │
     │ 6. Artifact                                                             │
     │                                                                         │
     │ validation_v3/CONTEXT_BUDGET_v3.md — frozen hashes, renderer            │
     │ version/hash, tokenizer identity and                                    │
     │ revision, context configuration, budget equation, prompt-family table,  │
     │ worst-case row, truncation                                              │
     │ scan, raw-fact-only proof, determinism, scope tests, all gate results.  │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Verification                                                            │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Budget: input_tokens + max_new_tokens <= usable_context for every case, │
     │ with max_new_tokens                                                     │
     │ reserved before acceptance. Over-budget raises; it never truncates.     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ No silent truncation — hard gate: a source scan of the Phase 6 code     │
     │ paths rejects                                                           │
     │ truncation=True, max_length= slicing, and string/row clipping.          │
     │ Tokenization is called without                                          │
     │ truncation and the gate asserts the decoded round trip is complete.     │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Raw facts only: rendered context is proven to contain no gold answer,   │
     │ gold SQL, generated QA,                                                 │
     │ evaluation label, answer_type, split, split_group or                    │
     │ certification_count — demonstrating that                                │
     │ oracle retrieval is not answer leakage.                                 │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Determinism: identical bytes across repeated renders and across a fresh │
     │ process.                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Worst-case row: report row_id, company, context tokens and              │
     │ complete-prompt tokens — the true                                       │
     │ maximum, not a cleaner-looking example, and with no gold answer         │
     │ exposed.                                                                │
     │                                                                         │
     │ Scope: every row rendered under each scope resolves only within that    │
     │ scope; cross-scope requests                                             │
     │ raise.                                                                  │

  4. Enforce the selected scope structurally                                    
                                                                                
  This is the most important Phase 7 leakage requirement.                       
                                                                                
  Model/gold SQL should remain scope-neutral and use logical table names such   
  as:                                                                           
                                                                                
  companies                                                                     
  processes                                                                     
  services                                                                      
  certifications                                                                
                                                                                
  Do not teach model-facing SQL:                                                
                                                                                
  train_kb_companies                                                            
  train_dev_kb_processes                                                        
  full_kb_services                                                              
                                                                                
  Those are infrastructure names.                                               
                                                                                
  At execution time, transparently bind the logical table names to the selected 
  scope's Phase 5 views.                                                        
                                                                                
  The implementation method may be chosen after inspecting the existing code,   
  but it must be robust — for example connection-local scoped aliases/views or  
  another deterministic mechanism. Do not use fragile textual replacement that  
  can be bypassed by aliases, quoting, CTEs, subqueries, or formatting.

  SELECT ... FROM companies

● Everything needed is mapped. One item in your instruction conflicts with the
  frozen protocol, and you asked me to stop and report rather than guess.

  What I found (all verified):
  - TEMP VIEW shadowing works robustly — SELECT ... FROM companies under
    scope=train_kb returns 148 via alias, subquery, CTE, and quoted name. That's
    the scope-binding mechanism.
  - Four bypasses must be blocked: main.companies → 205, full_kb_companies →
    205, sqlite_master → schema, and raw processes → 555 (so all four logical
    tables need shadowing).
  - mode=ro alone is insufficient: it blocks UPDATE/DELETE/CREATE, but ATTACH 
    and PRAGMA writable_schema are allowed — so PRAGMA query_only=ON plus
    statement validation is required.
  - v2's retired paths never existed in v3: no run_eval.py, sqlexec.py, or
    grade.py; no geo import, no max_rows=200. The only truncation=True strings
    are Phase 6's deliberate fault-test fixtures. So Phase 7 builds clean and 
    proves absence rather than removing.
  - The v2 bug is located: grade.py:170 — if not gold_set and not pred_set: 
    return 1.0. Empty prediction + empty gold scores 1.0.

     Phase 7 — SQL execution and grading

     Context

     Phase 6 is approved and frozen at 709ee1b. Phase 7 closes the three
     silent-failure paths README
     names — all of which "produce plausible numbers rather than errors" — and
     builds the scope-bound
     executor plus the task-aware grader.

     This is the highest-leverage gate in the protocol. README: it blocks the 
     canonical structured task
     pool, C and D generation, every structured probe, the Q42 revalidation, and
     final evaluation —
     "a probe built against a broken executor is worthless, and one scored by a
     broken grader is worse
     than worthless, because it produces a number."

     Pre-flight verified (read-only): branch main, HEAD 709ee1be…78d5, tree
     clean,
     gnem_v3.sqlite = 7437c746…b08b (matches).

     Conflict found and resolved: the request said Phase 8 is "Migrate the
     evaluation and reporting
     stack"; README.md:367 says "Build the v3 evaluation and reporting stack",
     and CLAUDE.md §33
     makes that substantive. User confirmed: use README's exact wording.

     Reconnaissance that determines the design

     Scope binding — TEMP VIEW shadowing works. SQLite resolves unqualified
     names in temp first, so
     a temp view named companies shadows main.companies. Verified under
     scope=train_kb: 148 rows
     via bare name, alias, subquery, CTE, and quoted "companies". This is robust
     where textual
     replacement is not.

     Four bypasses must be blocked (all verified reachable):

     ┌──────────────────────────────────────┬─────────┬────────────────────────┐
     │                bypass                │ returns │          must          │
     ├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────┼────────────────────────┤
     │ main.companies                       │ 205     │ reject                 │
     ├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────┼────────────────────────┤
     │ full_kb_companies (other scope's     │ 205     │ reject                 │
     │ physical view)                       │         │                        │
     ├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────┼────────────────────────┤
     │ sqlite_master                        │ schema  │ reject                 │
     ├──────────────────────────────────────┼─────────┼────────────────────────┤
     │ raw processes unshadowed             │ 555     │ shadow all four        │
     │                                      │         │ logical tables         │
     └──────────────────────────────────────┴─────────┴────────────────────────┘

     mode=ro is not enough. It blocks UPDATE/DELETE/CREATE, but ATTACH and
     PRAGMA writable_schema=ON are allowed, and PRAGMA query_only reads 0. So:
     mode=ro plus
     PRAGMA query_only=ON plus statement validation.

     v3 never inherited the retired paths. finetune/run_eval.py, sqlexec.py and
     grade.py do not
     exist; there is no geo import and no max_rows=200 anywhere. The only
     truncation=True strings are
     Phase 6's deliberate fault-test fixtures. So Phase 7 builds clean and 
     proves absence — the
     "minimal Phase 7 edit" to remove the 24,576 cut is zero edits, because the
     path was never ported.
     Phase 7 reuses the Phase 6 budget contract instead.

     The v2 bug is located: v2-frozen-reference:finetune/grade.py:170 —
     if not gold_set and not pred_set: return 1.0. Empty prediction + empty gold
     scores 1.0.

     Worth porting (§8 selective port): PRAGMA query_only = ON and mode=ro from
     v2:finetune/sqlexec.py:54-57. Not ported: the geo import (line 60),
     max_rows=200 (line 44),
     the hard-coded default db_path.

     Implementation

     1. CLAUDE.md live status block — lines 44-46 ONLY

     CURRENT_PHASE: 7, LAST_COMPLETED_PHASE: 6,
     NEXT_PHASE: Phase 8 — Build the v3 evaluation and reporting stack. §37
     example untouched.

     2. finetune/sqlexec_v3.py — scope-bound read-only executor

     - run_sql(sql, scope, *, db_path=None) — scope required, no default.
       Rejects omitted, None,
       "full", "FULL_KB", unknown, and non-string. DB path from config/caller;
       no hard-coded path, no
       v2 fallback.
     - Scope binding: open mode=ro, set PRAGMA query_only=ON, then create TEMP
       views shadowing all
       four logical names (companies, certifications, processes, services) onto
       that scope's
       Phase 5 views. Logical SQL stays scope-neutral; the executor chooses
       visibility.
     - Statement validation (defence in depth): single statement only;
       SELECT/WITH only; reject
       schema-qualified references (main., temp.), every physical scoped-view
       name, sqlite_master,
       PRAGMA, ATTACH, and all write verbs. Rejection is an explicit error —
       never a rewrite.
     - No result cap. Complete results always. README allows a ceiling only if
       it raises; since none
       is authorized and the KB is tiny, none is introduced — and no cap is
       added under another name.
     - No semantic rewriting: no added LIMIT, no clause removal, no SQL repair,
       no reprojection.

     3. finetune/grade_v3.py — task-aware grader

     - answer_type and target_columns come from item metadata, emitted by the
       generator. Missing
       or invalid → explicit failure, never inference from question wording, SQL
       shape, prediction text,
       or gold shape.
     - Frozen semantics: set projects target columns then dedupes, order
       irrelevant; scalar
       requires exactly one scalar-compatible column and one row; top_k
       preserves order and length and
       is never set-deduped; multi_part requires every part.
     - Two distinct metrics: task_result_correctness (primary) and
       strict_result_schema_accuracy (secondary), with fixtures where values are
       semantically right but
       the schema is wrong, proving they genuinely differ.
     - Statuses per CLAUDE.md §25: correct, incorrect, generation_failure,
       parse_failure,
       SQL_error, timeout, truncated_output, invalid_output — exactly one per
       item.
     - truncated_output when generation stopped at max_new_tokens: the partial
       text is never
       parsed as SQL or as an answer, and the status propagates.
     - Scope-pair invariant: a helper executes prediction and gold through one
       scope argument, and a
       mismatch raises loudly rather than scoring differently.

     4. finetune/phase7_grader_tests.py — unit + fault suite, and the audit

     Degenerate battery (empty, refusal, noise, malformed) including the v2 
     empty-gold regression;
     plus wrong column count, extra columns, duplicate set rows, top-k wrong
     order, top-k wrong length,
     incomplete multi-part, invalid/missing answer_type, invalid/missing
     target_columns; scope tests
     (148/165/205 and child membership); all four bypasses; write attempts with
     a DB-SHA check after;
     oversized prompt raising via the Phase 6 contract.

     5. Artifact

     validation_v3/GRADER_VALIDATION_v3.md — frozen DB hash, executor and grader
     versions/hashes,
     scope-binding mechanism, valid scopes, DB-path mechanism, absence proofs
     (geo import, max_rows,
     prompt truncation), answer-type and target-columns semantics, both metrics,
     the degenerate battery,
     truncated_output handling, read-only checks, and every unit/gate result.

     Verification

     train_kb/train_dev_kb/full_kb logical companies return 148 / 165 / 205;
     child tables
     likewise; all four bypasses rejected; scope mismatch between prediction and
     gold raises; a query
     returning all 205 rows returns 205, not 200; writes fail and the DB SHA is
     unchanged afterwards;
     degenerate predictions never score correct — including empty-vs-empty-gold;
     truncated_output is
     never parsed; both metrics computed and shown distinct; a source scan
     proves no geo import, no
     max_rows cap, no prompt truncation in Phase 7 paths.

     Out of scope (Phase 8+)

     No report.py, stats.py, error_analysis.py, verify.py, regrade.py, no
     evaluation/reporting
     stack, no holdout registry, exposure ledger, STRUCTURED_TASK_POOL_v3.jsonl,
     C/D datasets, probes,
     Q42, predictions, inference, adapters, training, routing, graph/geo.
     Test/Q42 remain
     LOCKED_UNTIL_PHASE_40. Phase 1–6 artifacts are not modified.

  Phase 7 is not formally accepted yet.                                         
                                                                                
  Codex found this structural-only bypass when lexical validation is disabled:  
                                                                                
  WITH companies AS (                                                           
    SELECT * FROM train_kb_companies                                            
  )                                                                             
  SELECT COUNT(*) FROM companies;

  instead of being denied structurally.                                         
                                                                                
  The normal run_sql path still rejects it lexically, so there is no immediate  
  live leakage path. However, Phase 7 explicitly claims:                        
                                                                                
  structural authorization = primary defense                                    
  lexical validation = defense in depth                                         
                                                                                
  Therefore the current implementation and audit are not yet correct.           
                                                                                
  1. Root cause to fix                                                          
                                                                                
  Inspect the authorizer logic around the current rule equivalent to:           
                                                                                
  source in LOGICAL_TABLES                                                      
                                                                                
  being treated as evidence that execution entered through an approved TEMP     
  logical binding.                                                              
                                                                                
  This assumption is invalid because SQLite may report a user-defined CTE       
  named:                                                                        
                                                                                
  companies                                                                     
  certifications                                                                
  processes                                                                     
  services                                                                      
                                                                                
  as source.                                                                    
                                                                                
  Therefore:                                                                    
                                                                                
  source == "companies"                                                         
                                                                                
  is not sufficient proof that the read originated from the trusted TEMP        
  companies binding.                                                            
                                                                                
  Do not patch only the exact query string above.                               
                                                                                
  Fix the structural distinction.                                               
                                                                                
  2. Required security property                                                 
                                                                                
  With lexical validation completely disabled, all of these must still be       
  structurally rejected:                                                        
                                                                                
  WITH companies AS (                                                           
    SELECT * FROM train_kb_companies                                            
  )                                                                             
  SELECT * FROM companies;                                                      
  WITH certifications AS (                                                      
    SELECT * FROM train_kb_certifications                                       
  )                                                                             
  SELECT * FROM certifications;                                                 
  WITH processes AS (                                                           
    SELECT * FROM train_kb_processes                                            
  )                                                                             
  SELECT * FROM processes;                                                      
  WITH services AS (                                                            
    SELECT * FROM train_kb_services                                             
  )                                                                             
  SELECT * FROM services;                                                       
                                                                                
  and equivalent variants for:                                                  
                                                                                
  train_dev_kb_*                                                                
  full_kb_*                                                                     
                                                                                
  as applicable.                                                                
                                                                                
  The structural layer must reject direct use of every physical scoped view,    
  even when hidden behind a CTE whose name matches one of the logical TEMP      
  table names.                                                                  
                                                                                
  3. Preserve legitimate SQL                                                    
                                                                                
  Do not solve this by rejecting all CTEs named:                                
                                                                                
  companies                                                                     
  certifications                                                                
  processes                                                                     
  services                                                                      
                                                                                
  if those names can legitimately occur without bypassing scope.

  SELECT COUNT(*) FROM companies;                                               
                                                                                
  aliases:                                                                      
                                                                                
  SELECT COUNT(*) FROM companies AS c;

● Phase: 7 — SQL execution and grading (correction)

  Correction status: FIXED — bypass closed by redesign, not a patch. 66/66
  checks pass.

  Root cause:
    The authorizer treated `source in LOGICAL_TABLES` as evidence that a read
  came
    from the trusted TEMP binding. That is unsound: SQLite reports a
  user-defined
    CTE named `companies` with source == "companies", IDENTICALLY to the trusted
    binding. Any rule keyed on the source NAME is therefore forgeable by naming
  a
    CTE after a logical table. Reproduced before fixing — the reported query
    returned 148 structurally. (The cross-scope variant, `WITH companies AS
    (SELECT * FROM full_kb_companies)` under train_kb, was already denied by the
    out-of-scope rule, so no cross-scope data ever leaked; the defect was that
    in-scope physical views were structurally reachable.)

  Files changed:
    finetune/sqlexec_v3.py                binding + authorizer + precheck
  redesign
    finetune/phase7_grader_tests.py       CTE-collision matrix + audit generator
    validation_v3/GRADER_VALIDATION_v3.md regenerated from the rerun
    grade_v3.py UNCHANGED · frozen DB and all Phase 1–6 artifacts UNCHANGED

  Structural fix:
    Scoped rows are now MATERIALIZED into temp tables, populated from the Phase
  5
    scoped views (which remain the single definition of scope membership). A
    legitimate query then reads only the temp schema and never touches `main`,
  so
    authorization collapses to one forgery-proof condition:

        legitimate read   READ db=None    (temp binding)   allow
        ANY bypass        READ db='main'                   DENY

    `source` is no longer consulted anywhere in the executor. The schema an
  object
    lives in cannot be forged by naming, so a raw base table, a physical view of
    any scope, or either hidden behind a CTE/alias/subquery is denied
  identically.
    Data fidelity verified: 12/12 scope×table combinations row- and
    column-identical to their Phase 5 views, including NULL city/county, integer
    employment, and the AVS row-16 trailing-space address.

  CTE-collision tests added — 19 vectors:
    12 = all four logical names (companies, certifications, processes, services)
         × all three scopes' physical views
     7 = nested chain · quoted identifiers · aliased · subquery-wrapped ·
         over main base table · two-CTE join collision · comment-obfuscated

    46 vectors total, all DENIED — 31 previous + 19 CTE collisions (4 overlap).
    Breakdown: 4 raw base tables via main.* · 12 physical scoped views ·
    19 CTE-name collisions · 4 schema tables · pragma_* TVFs · PRAGMA · ATTACH ·
    quoted/comment forms.

     /plan to edit
     Phase 8 — Build the v3 evaluation and reporting stack

     Context

     Phase 7 is approved and frozen at d8aedf9. Phase 8 builds the stack that
     will turn predictions into
     metrics, statistics, error analysis and a report — and, critically,
     establishes the architecture that
     keeps test/Q42 sealed until Phase 40.

     README Phase 8 is explicit that the v2 modules are "heavily wired to
     retired geo/graph/router/dose
     concepts" and that "recreating that entire architecture would add risk
     rather than remove it".
     CLAUDE.md §33 makes the gate functional: known-correct and known-wrong
     synthetic fixtures must pass
     through parsing, grading, statistics, error analysis and report generation
     without touching retired
     components.

     Pre-flight verified (read-only): branch main, HEAD d8aedf99…5f31c, tree
     clean, and all four
     frozen hashes match — DB 7437c746…b08b, executor a6c06b71…c461, grader
     619b9350…4cbd, Phase 7
     tests fefbd447…2692.

     Evidence for rebuild, not port

     ┌───────────────────┬───────┬──────────────────────┐
     │     v2 module     │ lines │ retired-concept refs │
     ├───────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ report.py         │ 1457  │ 111                  │
     ├───────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ error_analysis.py │ 872   │ 11                   │
     ├───────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ run_eval.py       │ 722   │ —                    │
     ├───────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ stats.py          │ 705   │ 9                    │
     ├───────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ regrade.py        │ 279   │ 4                    │
     ├───────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ verify.py         │ 178   │ 6                    │
     ├───────────────────┼───────┼──────────────────────┤
     │ total             │ 4213  │                      │
     └───────────────────┴───────┴──────────────────────┘

     report.py is unsalvageable as a unit. stats.py is the opposite case: its
     paired_bootstrap,
     hierarchical_bootstrap, effect_size and holm are exactly the methods README
     Phase 41 requires
     ("per-seed scores, mean ± SD, paired item-level bootstrap effect estimates,
     effect sizes and exact
     denominators… Holm correction"). Those methods are ported as concepts and
     reimplemented against
     the v3 record schema; no v2 file is copied.

     Contract points read from the live protocol

     - Status vocabulary (README Phase 40) is byte-identical to
       grade_v3.STATUSES, and the hard
       assert is scored + failed == expected probe size.
     - Retention (README Phase 40): example_id, question, raw output, parsed
       output, generated SQL,
       execution result, gold, score, error type, adapter hash, prompt hash —
       "Never scores alone."
     - Unseal (CLAUDE.md §23): "The evaluation entry point must require an
       explicit Phase-40-only
       --unseal control or equivalent."
     - Baselines are deterministic (Phase 41): never report a baseline SD, never
       pair a baseline
       "seed" with a training seed.

     Two conflicts found and resolved by the user

     1. README Phase 8 says emit "a REPORT_v3.md skeleton", but README:1020
        makes REPORT_v3.md the
        Phase 42 deliverable. The skeleton goes to
        validation_v3/fixtures_v3/REPORT_v3_skeleton.md
        with a synthetic banner; the repo-root path stays free until Phase 42.
     2. EVAL_STACK_VALIDATION_v3.md is not named in README or CLAUDE.md. It is
        created by
        convention, and the audit says so in-file.

     Implementation

     1. CLAUDE.md live status block — lines 44-46 ONLY

     CURRENT_PHASE: 8, LAST_COMPLETED_PHASE: 7, NEXT_PHASE: Phase 9 — Freeze the
     Holdout Registry and Fact Exposure Ledger (README:381 verbatim). §37
     example untouched.

     2. finetune/eval_records_v3.py — record schema + seal boundary

     - EvalRecord: the canonical per-item record carrying every README Phase 40
       retention field, plus
       answer_type / target_columns / both metrics / status. Synthetic values in
       Phase 8, but the
       schema must carry real records later. No field is invented that Phase 9
       owns.
     - Seal boundary. A result path is classified dev or sealed.
       load_results(path, ...) refuses
       a sealed path unless unseal=True is passed explicitly — no default, no
       env fallback, no hidden
       auto-unseal. Dev-facing reporting functions do not accept an unseal 
       parameter at all, so they
       are structurally incapable of reading sealed output.
     - sealed_metadata(path) returns only count / hash / path for pre-Phase-40
       use — never example text,
       answers, errors or scores.

     3. finetune/eval_stats_v3.py — statistical plumbing

     mean_sd, paired_bootstrap, effect_size, holm, mcnemar, and split-group
     weighting (average
     within group, then across groups unweighted). Baseline handling refuses to
     compute an SD for a
     deterministic condition. Interfaces preserved; Phase 39/41 decisions are
     not preempted, and no
     significance claim is made on fixture data.

     4. finetune/eval_report_v3.py — summary, families, error analysis, skeleton

     Summary JSON with exact denominators and every failure status counted;
     per-family metrics driven by a
     data-driven registry (adding a family requires no edit to a hard-coded
     REPORT_ORDER); error
     analysis grouping by status / family / answer_type; and the REPORT_v3.md
     skeleton renderer.

     5. finetune/eval_verify_v3.py — verification + regrade

     Verify: expected count, unique example_id, exactly-one-status, required
     fields, provenance present,
     no denominator loss, no duplicates, no silently dropped family. Regrade:
     retains the raw prediction,
     re-runs the frozen Phase 7 grader, emits a new record with grader
     version/hash provenance, and
     never mutates the raw prediction, repairs SQL, or changes gold.

     6. finetune/phase8_eval_stack_tests.py — fixtures, gate, audit

     ~20 deterministic fixtures covering every status,
     SET/SCALAR/TOP-K/MULTI-PART, task-correct/
     schema-wrong and task-wrong/schema-correct, a genuine valid empty result,
     and the fault cases.
     SQL-bearing fixtures call the approved Phase 7 executor; no second
     execution path is written.

     7. Artifacts

     validation_v3/EVAL_STACK_VALIDATION_v3.md          audit (convention-named;
     flagged in-file)
     validation_v3/fixtures_v3/summary_v3.json          fixture summary
     validation_v3/fixtures_v3/error_analysis_v3.json   fixture error analysis
     validation_v3/fixtures_v3/REPORT_v3_skeleton.md    synthetic,
     banner-labelled

     Verification

     Fixture input accepted; correct/incorrect preserved; all eight statuses
     preserved; scored + failed == expected exact; summary JSON and per-family
     metrics correct; both metrics stay distinct in both
     directions; error analysis correct; stats plumbing correct on synthetic
     values; verify catches a bad
     fixture; regrade reproduces metrics with provenance; skeleton renders; a 
     sealed fixture cannot be
     read by dev reporting and unseal defaults to off; no geo/graph/router/dose
     dependency and no v2
     runtime path; deterministic rerun byte-identical.

     Fault tests (must fail loudly): duplicate example_id, missing required
     field, invalid status,
     expected-count mismatch, denominator mismatch, unsupported family silently
     dropped, attempted sealed
     analysis, corrupted raw prediction, mismatched grader provenance, stale
     result schema.

     Out of scope (Phase 9+)

     No holdout registry, exposure ledger, value/operation/compositional
     holdouts, few-shot eligibility or
     logical fingerprints; no Phase 10+ datasets; no probes; no Q42
     revalidation; no inference, adapters
     or training; no routing or graph/geo. Test/Q42 remain
     LOCKED_UNTIL_PHASE_40, validated with
     synthetic sealed fixtures only. Phase 1–7 artifacts are not modified.
